# 2026-10-08 ƒ?" FIX-153 local-candle hardening status

- Implemented: OFF-mode trader-owned candle ingestion, monotonic close tokens,
  bounded detector/lanes, canonical price evidence, direct-attempt freshness,
  REST exchange-time close validation, and controlled ADMIN drain behavior.
- Operational impact: a successful drain exits the JVM; a timeout resumes
  admission. Price protection pauses during a drain window.
- Database impact: existing FIX-153 V106/B105 schema remains required; no
  production migration or data change was run by Codex.
- Release status: NOT APPROVED FOR PRODUCTION. Java 21, disposable-MySQL,
  crash/race, Windows lock, soak and Replay-parity validation remain open.

# 2026-10-08 ƒ?" FIX-153 exchange/database clock correction

- Problem: JVM/database clock skew could make a Binance-derived candle appear
  too early or too old during freshness checks.
- Solution: `ExchangeClockSynchronizer` measures Binance server time against
  database UTC and JVM time at startup and periodically; database and JVM
  callers apply their own bounded offset without HTTP inside locks.
- The clock-skew warning uses the larger absolute database or JVM offset; the
  dedicated Binance time client has a five-second connect/read bound.
- General Binance REST requests now use the configured request timeout, and
  scheduled clock sampling cannot monopolise the four-thread scheduler pool.
- Bootstrap REST reads occur before its local candle-write transaction, so
  the bounded Binance timeout does not hold a database transaction open.

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-153 audit: analysis outcomes and protection clock

- Audit result: normal live and historical analysis persist a `TradeSignal`,
  including neutral/no-entry decisions; there is no normal analysed-without-
  durable-outcome path to reconcile.
- Corrected the post-lock direct-protection timestamp validation to use the
  JVM-to-exchange clock correction, matching initial receipt validation.
- A universal shutdown barrier remains a separate, unverified design task:
  it must cover financial mutations without treating unrelated administration
  or configuration writes as trading authority.
- Database impact: none. Production and Replay impact: execution freshness now
  follows an automatically measured exchange-aligned reference clock.
- Validation still required: unavailable-time-source, stale observation,
  positive/negative skew, and first-attempt freshness regression tests.

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-154 identifier alignment

- The pending higher-timeframe BUY-validation design was renumbered from
  `FIX-146` to `FIX-154` in the project guidance and architecture record.
- It remains separate from FIX-153, unimplemented, and not approved for
  deployment. There is no database, Production, or Replay behavior change.

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" Automated local-candle reconciliation direction

- Approved direction: the pending local-candle reconciler must automatically
  repair authoritative interrupted-open candles and exact absent identities in
  the active quality window. Inserted identities use `BACKFILL` provenance and
  never receive a close token or dispatch live analysis.
- No manual approval, database repair, or persisted review fence may be needed
  for pending candle/analysis reconciliation. Unverified data remains explicit
  non-executable evidence; this direction does not override legitimate
  strategy or risk-entry vetoes.
- Status: design only; no database migration, code, Production, or Replay
  behavior has changed.

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" Appendix K reconciliation contract alignment

- Project guidance now records the approved pending design constraints: pure
  insert-only authoritative gap backfill; quality-window-bounded candidates;
  durable terminal outcomes; structurally isolated historical processing; a
  shared Binance REST rate-limit gate; and durable financial-effect
  reconciliation only after atomicity is proven.
- The planned universal mutation barrier is explicitly in-memory and active
  only during drain; it must not queue, delay, or persistently fence fresh
  trading outside drain. Protection for open positions during replacement is
  still an unresolved release gate.
- Status: documentation/design alignment only. No runtime behavior, schema,
  Production data, or Replay behavior was changed.

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-153 automatic reconciliation implementation (initial slice)

- Problem: reconnect-boundary or startup candle gaps could leave local history
  invalid until a person performed a database repair.
- Solution: added V107 durable reconciliation/audit and terminal-outcome
  tables; guarded update-only authoritative recovery; pure insert-only gap
  backfill; a dedicated recovery executor; reconnect/scheduled requests;
  token-free recovery outcomes; post-ingestion late-live-close observation;

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-153 reconciliation outcome correction

- Corrected recovery semantics so `SKIPPED_NOT_LIVE` is recorded only when a
  recovery/backfill actually changes candle evidence; a competing live or
  existing row is not relabelled.
- `REST_NOT_FINAL` is now retryable rather than a terminal mismatch. Audit keys
  use fixed epoch-millisecond identity precision, and reconciliation is safely
  disabled by default even when configuration is absent.
- Added terminal outcomes for worker data-unavailable, historical-only,
  already-analysed, stale work, and detector expiry paths. Transient failures
  remain without a terminal outcome so retry remains possible.
- Added `LocalReconciliationAuditRepositoryTest`; targeted Java 21 tests and
  compile passed. MySQL, race/crash, rate-limit, barrier, effect-reconciler,
  and Replay validation remain open.

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-153 reconciliation safety correction

- Recovery now requires positive loss evidence before it may finalise an open
  row: either the successor candle is already present or a reconnect was
  admitted after the candidate close. This prevents a short healthy-stream
  delay from being converted into a token-free, unanalysed close.
- REST finality/identity disagreement is retryable while the local row remains
  open. The authoritative Binance close timestamp, not the local expected
  value, is persisted after a successful exact match. A bounded first-candle
  probe identifies pre-listing identities as explicit unavailable outcomes.
- Recovery uses the short timeout client and checks drain between requests;
  late-live-close evidence now preserves its first timestamp.
- Status: Java 21 compilation passed. Dedicated reconciliation race, drain,
  MySQL, and Replay tests remain required before enabling the feature.

# 2026-10-09 ƒ?" FIX-153 deterministic reconciliation test coverage

  successor evidence permits update-only repair and records a non-live outcome;
  a gap-insert race ends as `ALREADY_PRESENT` without inventing an outcome;
  a confirmed exchange gap ends as `SKIPPED_DATA_UNAVAILABLE`; and active drain
  prevents REST admission.

# 2026-10-10 ƒ?" FIX-154 trusted higher-timeframe BUY assessment (implementation)

- **Problem:** every bearish 1h row had equal veto authority, including
  historical rows with unknown data-quality provenance; there was no durable
  explanation of the effective 5m/1h BUY context.
- **Root cause:** BUY validation and consolidation read raw `TradeSignal`
  decisions directly, with no assessment of quality or bearish strength.
- **Solution:** added `data_quality_valid` persistence to `TradeSignal` and
  `AnalysisService`; introduced `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`; wired it
  into direct BUY, accumulated-evidence authority, consolidation and
  observational progressive-add evidence. The SOFT 1h result is capped at 25%
  and uses immutable effective decisions; HARD and unknown bearish evidence
  vetoes. `V107` creates `data_quality_valid` and append-only
  `buy_htf_assessment_audit`.
- **Files/classes:** `TradeSignal`, `AnalysisService`,
  `HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService`, `TradeExecutionValidationService`,
  `OpportunityConsolidationService`, `ExecutionIntelligenceService`,
  `BuyHtfAssessmentAuditStore`, `V107__fix_154_trusted_higher_timeframe_buy.sql`,
  and focused tests.
- **Database impact:** new nullable `trade_signal.data_quality_valid`; no
  backfill. Historical `NULL` stays unknown. New append-only audit table.
- **Production / Replay impact:** the same shared validation service is used
  for both. Audit failure is explicitly non-blocking. V108 ships because live
  runtime outcome writers require its schema; reconciliation remains disabled.
- **Validation status:** focused Maven tests could not run in this shell because
  the active JDK reports `release version 21 not supported`; execute with the
  project Java 21 toolchain before release.

## Validation correction ƒ?" 2026-10-10

The shell was switched to the installed Java 21 toolchain
(`C:\Program Files\Java\jdk-21`). Focused Maven verification then passed:
`HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentServiceTest`,
`TradeExecutionValidationServiceTest`, and
`OpportunityConsolidationServiceTest`.

## FIX-154 review-round correction ƒ?" 2026-10-10

- **Replay parity:** V107 now brings `trade_signal_test` forward with the
  V97 BTC freshness fields, V106 `signal_mode`, and FIX-154
  `data_quality_valid`, matching the reflection-based Replay writer.
- **Audit isolation:** audit records are snapshot and submitted to a bounded,
  one-thread executor. Replay never writes a Production audit row, and audit
  saturation/failure only increments an in-memory metric.
- **Evidence accuracy:** audit candle close times are derived from candle open
  time plus the interval, hard-confirmation is persisted, and both legacy veto
  codes are retained.
- **Startup safety:** outcome-schema verification now occurs at bean creation;
  FIX-154 verifies its required production and replay columns before runtime
  admission.
- **Validation:** Java 21 focused tests passed, including the higher-timeframe
  matrix, validation, consolidation and early outcome-schema verifier tests.

- Added explicit SOFT-policy tests for conservative/direct/context/consolidated
  sizing, neutral-5m non-escalation, untrusted non-bearish context, quality
  boundaries, and Replay audit suppression.

  is run.
- Added read-only FIX-154 health counters at `/api/fix154/health`.
- The MySQL test remains an external release-stage proof: it cannot run in this
  session without the designated disposable MySQL environment variables.

## FIX-153 automatic reconciliation enabled ƒ?" 2026-10-10

- Problem: a missing historical candle left current candle ingestion healthy
  but blocked later signal generation for the affected lane.
- Root cause: the data-quality gate correctly rejects incomplete windows; the
  recovery component was present but disabled.
- Solution: enabled OFF-mode token-free reconciliation, kept its one-worker
  executor as the default, and added deterministic tests for reconnect
  evidence, bad local close-time replacement, and per-pass REST budgeting.
- Files changed: `application.yml`, `LocalCandleReconciliationConfig`, and
  `LocalCandleReconciliationServiceTest`.
- Database impact: uses existing V108 audit/outcome tables; no data rewrite or
  backfill was executed by this change.
- Production/Replay impact: recovery is non-executable and has no Replay
  authority. Targeted Java 21 reconciliation tests passed; real-MySQL release
  tests still require the disposable test environment.

  66:     // FIX-087: heartbeat age alone cannot prove that a long replay worker has died.
  67:     // Some deterministic replay stages can legitimately run for more than the stale-heartbeat
  68:     // threshold without returning to updateRun(). Keep atomic in-JVM ownership so Resume cannot
  69:     // launch a second worker for the same durable run while the first worker is still executing.
  70:     private final Set<Long> activeRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  71: 
  72:     // FIX-090: cooperative stop requests are tracked separately from active ownership. A Stop Test
  73:     // request never pretends the worker is gone; Delete Data stays blocked until runAsync reaches
  74:     // finally and removes the run from activeRunIds.
  75:     private final Set<Long> stopRequestedRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  76: 
  77:     public boolean isActive(long runId) {
  78:         return activeRunIds.contains(runId);
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     public boolean hasActiveRuns() {
  82:         return !activeRunIds.isEmpty();
  83:     }
  84: 
  85:     public boolean requestStop(long runId) {
  86:         if (!activeRunIds.contains(runId)) return false;
  87:         stopRequestedRunIds.add(runId);
  88:         return true;
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     public boolean isStopRequested(long runId) {
  92:         return stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId);
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     private void checkStopRequested(long runId) {
  96:         if (stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId)) throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     @Async
 100:     public void runAsync(long runId, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
 101:         // FIX-087: close the race at worker entry as well. Even if two callers schedule the same
 102:         // run almost simultaneously, only one may execute the deterministic replay pipeline.
 103:         if (!activeRunIds.add(runId)) {
 104:             log.warn("FIX-087: replay run {} already has an active worker; duplicate execution skipped", runId);
 105:             return;
 106:         }
 107:         // FIX-11J: Replay-only observational timers. The worker pipeline below is intentionally
 108:         // left in its existing order; these locals only capture elapsed monotonic time and are
 109:         // persisted once in finally so diagnostics cannot steer or fail Replay business logic.
 110:         final long replayTotalStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 111:         Long loadHistoricalDataNs = null;
 112:         Long verifyEventResolutionNs = null;
 113:         Long buildReplayDatasetNs = null;
 114:         Long generateFreshSignalsNs = null;
 115:         Long shadowExecutionNs = null;
 116:         Long parityComparisonNs = null;
 117:         try (var inputAudit = sharedMarketEnabled ? com.crypto.shared.CandleInputAudit.open(jdbcTemplate,auditTransactionManager,"REPLAY:"+runId) : null) {
 118:             long stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 119:             Map<String, Object> run = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap(
 120:                     "SELECT symbol, start_time, end_time FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id = ?", runId);
 121:             String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol"));
 122:             Instant start = toInstant(run.get("start_time"));
 123:             Instant end = toInstant(run.get("end_time"));
 124: 
 125:             updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 2, "Loading historical candles and signals", null);
 126:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 127: 
 128:             Instant contextStart = start.minus(REPLAY_CONTEXT_WARMUP);
 129:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles = candles(symbol, "1m", contextStart, end);
 130:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles = candles(symbol, "5m", contextStart, end);
 131:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles = candles(symbol, "1h", contextStart, end);
 132:             List<Candle> requestedOneMinuteCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(oneMinuteCandles, "1m", start, end);
 133:             List<Candle> requestedFiveMinuteCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(fiveMinuteCandles, "5m", start, end);
 134:             List<Candle> requestedOneHourCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(oneHourCandles, "1h", start, end);
 135: 
 136:             String btcSymbol = btcContextProperties.referenceSymbol();
 137:             boolean replayBtcContext = btcContextProperties.enabled() && !btcSymbol.equalsIgnoreCase(symbol);
 138:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "1m", contextStart, end) : List.of();
 139:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "5m", contextStart, end) : List.of();
 140:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "1h", contextStart, end) : List.of();
 141: 
 142:             List<TradeSignal> sourceSignals = signalRepository
 143:                     .findBySymbolAndGeneratedAtBetweenOrderByGeneratedAtAsc(symbol, start, end);
 144: 
 145:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET source_signal_count=? WHERE id=?",
 146:                     sourceSignals.size(), runId);
 147:             loadHistoricalDataNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
 148: 
 149:             stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 150:             updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 10, "Replaying event-candle resolution (no production writes)", null);
 151:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 152:             int replay1m = verifyEventResolution(runId, symbol, "1m", requestedOneMinuteCandles, 10, 28);
 153:             int replay5m = verifyEventResolution(runId, symbol, "5m", requestedFiveMinuteCandles, 28, 38);
 154:             int replay1h = verifyEventResolution(runId, symbol, "1h", requestedOneHourCandles, 38, 42);
 155:             verifyEventResolutionNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
 156: 
 157:             updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 43, "Generating fresh signals from historical candles", null);
 158:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 159:             // FIX-11H: OLD=DATABASE and NEW=DATASET are explicit replay-only modes. Production
 160:             // has no dependency on this selector. verifyEventResolution() intentionally remains unchanged.
 161:             ReplayDataset replayDataset;
 162:             if (dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET) {
 163:                 stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 164:                 replayDataset = loadDataset(symbol, btcSymbol, contextStart, end);
 165:                 buildReplayDatasetNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
 166:             } else {
 167:                 // DATABASE mode has no ReplayDataset build stage. Persist NULL so the UI can
 168:                 // distinguish "not applicable" from a fabricated zero-duration measurement.
 169:                 replayDataset = null;
 170:             }
 171:             log.info("FIX-11H replay data source: run={}, mode={}, symbol={}, start={}, end={}",
 172:                     runId, dataSource, symbol, start, end);
 173:             stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 174:             FreshReplayStats fresh = generateFreshSignals(
 175:                     runId, symbol, start, end,
 176:                     oneMinuteCandles, fiveMinuteCandles, oneHourCandles,
 177:                     btcSymbol, btcOneMinuteCandles, btcFiveMinuteCandles, btcOneHourCandles,
 178:                     dataSource, replayDataset);
 179:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 180:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 181:                     SET generated_signal_count=?, generated_buy_count=?, generated_watch_count=?,
 182:                         generated_sell_count=?, generated_strong_sell_count=?
 183:                     WHERE id=?
 184:                     """, fresh.total(), fresh.buys(), fresh.watches(), fresh.sells(), fresh.strongSells(), runId);
 185:             generateFreshSignalsNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
 186: 
 187:             stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 188:             updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 74, "Running full shadow-production execution flow", null);
 189:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 190:             // FIX-052: Replay position protection consumes the same canonical live 1m
 191:             // price observations that Production consumed. Historical windows before
 192:             // V64 was deployed naturally have no events and are explicitly degraded to
 193:             // signal/candle-close protection inside ShadowProductionReplayService.
 194:             // FIX-153: validate the entire price window consumed, including context warm-up.
 195:             marketPriceEventService.assertReplayEvidence(symbol,contextStart,end);
 196:             var priceOutcomeCounts=marketPriceEventService.replayOutcomeCounts(symbol,contextStart,end);
 197:             List<MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent> productionPriceEvents =
 198:                     marketPriceEventService.find(symbol, contextStart, end);
 199:             ShadowProductionReplayService.ReplayStats shadow = shadowReplayService.replay(
 200:                     runId, symbol, start, end, fresh.generatedSignals(), productionPriceEvents,
 201:                     () -> isStopRequested(runId));
 202:             // FIX-109: persist the parity contract with the run. A SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK
 203:             // result is useful historical evidence, but must never be presented as tick-exact
 204:             // Production reproduction. Production parity is the default logic mode.
 205:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET replay_price_mode=?, replay_logic_mode=? WHERE id=?",
 206:                     shadow.priceReplayMode(), shadow.logicMode(), runId);
 207:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 208:             shadowExecutionNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
 209: 
 210:             stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 211:             updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 82, "Comparing historical decision authority", null);
 212:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 213:             int authorityCorrections = 0;
 214:             int replaySignals = 0;
 215:             int oldHardReversals = 0;
 216:             int correctedHardReversals = 0;
 217: 
 218:             for (TradeSignal signal : sourceSignals) {
 219:                 checkStopRequested(runId);
 220:                 SignalDecision finalDecision = signal.getDecision();
 221:                 SignalDecision original = signal.getOriginalDecision();
 222:                 SignalDecision effective = finalDecision != null ? finalDecision : original;
 223:                 boolean corrected = finalDecision != null && original != null
 224:                         && isBearish(original) && !isBearish(finalDecision);
 225:                 if (corrected) authorityCorrections++;
 226: 
 227:                 // Historical rows are reference-only; fresh replay rows are persisted separately.
 228:                 replaySignals++;
 229: 
 230:                 if ("1m".equals(signal.getInterval())) {
 231:                     TradeSignal five = signalRepository
 232:                             .findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(
 233:                                     symbol, "5m", signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 234:                     TradeSignal one = signalRepository
 235:                             .findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(
 236:                                     symbol, "1h", signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 237: 
 238:                     SignalDecision fiveDecision = five == null ? null : five.getDecision();
 239:                     SignalDecision oneDecision = one == null ? null : one.getDecision();
 240:                     boolean higherBearish = isBearish(fiveDecision) || isBearish(oneDecision);
 241:                     boolean oldHard = finalDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
 242:                             || original == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
 243:                             || higherBearish;
 244:                     boolean newHard = finalDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL || higherBearish;
 245:                     if (oldHard) oldHardReversals++;
 246:                     if (newHard) correctedHardReversals++;
 247: 
 248:                     String code;
 249:                     String explanation;
 250:                     if (oldHard && !newHard) {
 250:                     if (oldHard && !newHard) {
 251:                         code = "ORIGINAL_BEARISH_NEUTRALIZED";
 252:                         explanation = "Old execution logic would hard-cancel because originalDecision was STRONG_SELL, "
 253:                                 + "but FinalDecisionService neutralized it and higher timeframes are not bearish.";
 254:                     } else if (newHard) {
 255:                         code = "BEARISH_REVERSAL";
 256:                         explanation = "Corrected hard reversal remains valid from the final decision or final 5m/1h context.";
 257:                     } else {
 258:                         code = "NO_HARD_REVERSAL";
 259:                         explanation = "No hard bearish reversal under corrected final-decision authority.";
 260:                     }
 261: 
 262:                     jdbcTemplate.update("""
 263:                             INSERT INTO execution_opportunity_test
 264:                                 (test_run_id, source_signal_id, symbol, generated_at,
 265:                                  current_final_decision, current_original_decision,
 266:                                  five_minute_decision, one_hour_decision,
 267:                                  old_hard_bearish_reversal, corrected_hard_bearish_reversal,
 268:                                  decision_code, decision_explanation)
 269:                             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
 270:                             """,
 271:                             runId,
 272:                             signal.getId(),
 273:                             symbol,
 274:                             Timestamp.from(signal.getGeneratedAt()),
 275:                             finalDecision == null ? null : finalDecision.name(),
 276:                             original == null ? null : original.name(),
 277:                             fiveDecision == null ? null : fiveDecision.name(),
 278:                             oneDecision == null ? null : oneDecision.name(),
 279:                             oldHard,
 280:                             newHard,
 281:                             code,
 282:                             explanation
 283:                     );
 284:                 }
 285:             }
 286: 
 287:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 288:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 289:                     SET replay_signal_count=?, neutralized_original_bearish_count=?,
 290:                         historical_hard_reversal_count=?, corrected_hard_reversal_count=?
 291:                     WHERE id=?
 292:                     """, replaySignals, authorityCorrections, oldHardReversals, correctedHardReversals, runId);
 293: 
 294:             updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 94, "Calculating regression result", null);
 295:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 296: 
 297:             int historical1m = countSignals(sourceSignals, "1m");
 298:             int historical5m = countSignals(sourceSignals, "5m");
 299:             int historical1h = countSignals(sourceSignals, "1h");
 300:             int requestedCandles1m = requestedOneMinuteCandles.size();
 301:             int requestedCandles5m = requestedFiveMinuteCandles.size();
 302:             int requestedCandles1h = requestedOneHourCandles.size();
 303:             // FIX-11O: distinguish Replay generation cadence from historical source/event metadata coverage.
 304:             // A stale/missing Production candle-close marker must remain visible as a diagnostic warning,
 305:             // but it must not make a fully generated Replay fail when every requested timeframe candle
 306:             // produced a fresh signal with zero generation errors. This is Replay/test status only and
 307:             // cannot alter Production analysis, execution, persistence, wallet state, or trading decisions.
 308:             boolean historicalEventCoveragePass = replay1m == requestedCandles1m
 309:                     && replay5m == requestedCandles5m
 310:                     && replay1h == requestedCandles1h;
 311:             boolean cadencePass = fresh.oneMinuteSignals() == requestedCandles1m
 312:                     && fresh.fiveMinuteSignals() == requestedCandles5m
 313:                     && fresh.oneHourSignals() == requestedCandles1h;
 314:             boolean authorityPass = sourceSignals.stream().allMatch(s -> {
 315:                 SignalDecision effective = s.getDecision() != null ? s.getDecision() : s.getOriginalDecision();
 316:                 return s.getDecision() == null || effective == s.getDecision();
 317:             });
 318:             boolean generationPass = fresh.errors() == 0 && fresh.total() > 0;
 319:             // FIX-052: an "exact Production" replay must include the same live 1m price
 320:             // observations used by LivePositionProtectionService. Do not report a green
 321:             // parity result for older windows that only have candle-close approximations.
 322:             boolean livePriceParityPass = !productionPriceEvents.isEmpty();
 323:             boolean passed = cadencePass && authorityPass && generationPass && livePriceParityPass;
 324: 
 325:             String notes = "Fresh replay signals are generated from historical candles through TechnicalIndicatorService "
 326:                     + "and the production AnalysisService scoring/final-decision path without trade_signal persistence. "
 327:                     + "A three-hour context-only warm-up seeds 1m/5m/1h state before the requested execution window, and BTC reference signals are freshly replayed as-of each timestamp when BTC context is enabled. "
 328:                     + "Historical signal counts are retained only as the pre-fix reference. "
 329:                     + (historicalEventCoveragePass
 330:                         ? "Historical event-resolution coverage is complete for the requested 1m/5m/1h candles. "
 331:                         : "FIX-11O source-data warning: historical event-resolution coverage is incomplete (1m "
 332:                             + replay1m + "/" + requestedCandles1m + ", 5m " + replay5m + "/" + requestedCandles5m
 333:                             + ", 1h " + replay1h + "/" + requestedCandles1h + "). Fresh Replay cadence is evaluated "
 334:                             + "from generated signals instead, so an old Production persistence/closed-marker gap remains visible without hiding valid Replay BUY/SELL output. ")
 335:                     + (livePriceParityPass
 336:                         ? "FIX-052/FIX-056 exact price parity is active: Replay consumed " + productionPriceEvents.size() + " persisted Production 1m live-price observations in UTC order, exposes the latest event through the shared ExecutionPriceAuthorityService, and revalidates/sizes BUYs from that execution-time price. "
 337:                         : "FIX-052/FIX-056 exact price parity is NOT available for this historical window because no persisted Production live-price observations exist; the run is intentionally not marked fully passed. ")
 338:                     + "FIX-153 persisted price outcomes in consumed window (NON_CANONICAL/PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED are excluded, unresolved PENDING aborts): " + priceOutcomeCounts + ". "
 339:                     + "The decision replay validates that originalDecision is audit-only and cannot override a non-null final decision. "
 340:                     + "Regression AnalysisService returns unsaved TradeSignal objects. Fresh signals then pass through an isolated shadow execution/position lifecycle that records exact simulated BUY/SELL points. Real wallet, trade_signal and production execution_opportunity tables are never written.";
 341: 
 342:             // FIX-087: one result row per run is an invariant. Worker ownership prevents the
 343:             // overlap at its source; this idempotent write is the database-level last safety net
 344:             // so a duplicate completion can never turn an otherwise valid replay into ERROR.
 345:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 346:                     INSERT INTO analysis_test_result
 347:                         (test_run_id,
 348:                          candles_1m, signals_1m_historical, replayable_1m_events, generated_signals_1m, generated_buys_1m,
 349:                          candles_5m, signals_5m_historical, replayable_5m_events, generated_signals_5m, generated_buys_5m,
 350:                          candles_1h, signals_1h_historical, replayable_1h_events, generated_signals_1h, generated_buys_1h,
 351:                          generated_signal_errors, simulated_trades, simulated_wins, simulated_losses,
 352:                          simulated_realized_pnl, simulated_final_wallet,
 353:                          decision_authority_corrections, old_hard_bearish_reversals,
 354:                          corrected_hard_bearish_reversals, cadence_path_passed, decision_authority_passed, test_passed, notes)
 355:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
 356:                     ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
 357:                         candles_1m=VALUES(candles_1m),
 358:                         signals_1m_historical=VALUES(signals_1m_historical),
 359:                         replayable_1m_events=VALUES(replayable_1m_events),
 360:                         generated_signals_1m=VALUES(generated_signals_1m),
 361:                         generated_buys_1m=VALUES(generated_buys_1m),
 362:                         candles_5m=VALUES(candles_5m),
 363:                         signals_5m_historical=VALUES(signals_5m_historical),
 364:                         replayable_5m_events=VALUES(replayable_5m_events),
 365:                         generated_signals_5m=VALUES(generated_signals_5m),
 366:                         generated_buys_5m=VALUES(generated_buys_5m),
 367:                         candles_1h=VALUES(candles_1h),
 368:                         signals_1h_historical=VALUES(signals_1h_historical),
 369:                         replayable_1h_events=VALUES(replayable_1h_events),
 370:                         generated_signals_1h=VALUES(generated_signals_1h),
 371:                         generated_buys_1h=VALUES(generated_buys_1h),
 372:                         generated_signal_errors=VALUES(generated_signal_errors),
 373:                         simulated_trades=VALUES(simulated_trades),
 374:                         simulated_wins=VALUES(simulated_wins),
 375:                         simulated_losses=VALUES(simulated_losses),
 376:                         simulated_realized_pnl=VALUES(simulated_realized_pnl),
 377:                         simulated_final_wallet=VALUES(simulated_final_wallet),
 378:                         decision_authority_corrections=VALUES(decision_authority_corrections),
 379:                         old_hard_bearish_reversals=VALUES(old_hard_bearish_reversals),
 380:                         corrected_hard_bearish_reversals=VALUES(corrected_hard_bearish_reversals),
 381:                         cadence_path_passed=VALUES(cadence_path_passed),
 382:                         decision_authority_passed=VALUES(decision_authority_passed),
 383:                         test_passed=VALUES(test_passed),
 384:                         notes=VALUES(notes)
 385:                     """,
 386:                     runId,
 387:                     requestedCandles1m, historical1m, replay1m, fresh.oneMinuteSignals(), fresh.oneMinuteBuys(),
 388:                     requestedCandles5m, historical5m, replay5m, fresh.fiveMinuteSignals(), fresh.fiveMinuteBuys(),
 389:                     requestedCandles1h, historical1h, replay1h, fresh.oneHourSignals(), fresh.oneHourBuys(),
 390:                     fresh.errors(), shadow.trades(), shadow.wins(), shadow.losses(), shadow.realizedPnl(), shadow.finalWallet(),
 391:                     authorityCorrections, oldHardReversals, correctedHardReversals,
 392:                     cadencePass, authorityPass, passed, notes
 393:             );
 394:             parityComparisonNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
 395: 
 396:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 397:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 398:                     SET status=?, progress_percent=100, current_step=?, heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6), completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 399:                     WHERE id=?
 400:                     """, passed ? "PASSED" : "FAILED", passed ? "Regression checks passed" : "Regression checks failed", runId);
 401: 
 402:         } catch (ReplayCancellationException stop) {
 403:             // FIX-090: only mark ERROR after the replay pipeline has actually unwound to here.
 404:             // This makes ERROR a trustworthy signal that Delete Data may proceed once active_worker=false.
 405:             log.warn("FIX-090: regression test run {} stopped by user request", runId);
 406:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 407:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 408:                     SET status='ERROR', current_step='Stopped by user',
 409:                         error_message='Replay stopped manually by user. Test data may now be deleted.',
 410:                         failure_step='Manual stop', failure_exception=NULL, failure_root_cause=NULL,
 411:                         failure_stack_trace=NULL, heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6), completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 412:                     WHERE id=?
 413:                     """, runId);
 414:         } catch (Exception exception) {
 415:             log.error("Regression test run {} failed", runId, exception);
 416:             String failedStep = currentStep(runId);
 417:             String summary = errorDetail(exception, 1900);
 418:             Throwable root = rootCause(exception);
 419:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 420:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 421:                     SET status='ERROR', progress_percent=100, current_step='Regression test failed', heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 422:                         error_message=?, failure_step=?, failure_exception=?, failure_root_cause=?,
 423:                         failure_stack_trace=?, started_at=COALESCE(started_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 424:                         completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 425:                     WHERE id=?
 426:                     """,
 427:                     summary, failedStep, exception.getClass().getName(),
 428:                     rootCauseDetail(root, 1000), stackTrace(exception), runId);
 429:         } finally {
 430:             // FIX-11J: persist one timing snapshot after the existing Replay path has completed or
 431:             // unwound. Diagnostics persistence is deliberately best-effort: it must never replace
 432:             // the Replay's real PASSED/FAILED/ERROR outcome or swallow the original exception.
 433:             long replayTotalNs = System.nanoTime() - replayTotalStartedNs;
 434:             persistReplayTimingsSafely(runId, loadHistoricalDataNs, verifyEventResolutionNs,
 435:                     buildReplayDatasetNs, generateFreshSignalsNs, shadowExecutionNs,
 436:                     parityComparisonNs, replayTotalNs);
 437: 
 438:             // FIX-087: always release ownership, including ERROR paths, so a genuinely stopped
 439:             // replay remains recoverable later using the same run id and original replay window.
 440:             activeRunIds.remove(runId);
 441:             stopRequestedRunIds.remove(runId);
 442:         }
 443:     }
 444: 
 445:     private void persistReplayTimingsSafely(long runId, Long loadHistoricalDataNs,
 446:                                             Long verifyEventResolutionNs, Long buildReplayDatasetNs,
 447:                                             Long generateFreshSignalsNs, Long shadowExecutionNs,
 448:                                             Long parityComparisonNs, long replayTotalNs) {
 449:         try {
 450:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 451:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 452:                     SET timing_load_historical_ns=?, timing_verify_event_resolution_ns=?,
 453:                         timing_build_replay_dataset_ns=?, timing_generate_fresh_signals_ns=?,
 454:                         timing_shadow_execution_ns=?, timing_parity_comparison_ns=?, timing_total_ns=?
 455:                     WHERE id=?
 456:                     """, loadHistoricalDataNs, verifyEventResolutionNs, buildReplayDatasetNs,
 457:                     generateFreshSignalsNs, shadowExecutionNs, parityComparisonNs, replayTotalNs, runId);
 458:         } catch (Exception timingPersistenceFailure) {
 459:             log.error("FIX-11J: unable to persist Replay timing diagnostics for run {}",
 460:                     runId, timingPersistenceFailure);
 461:         }
 462:     }
 463: 
 464:     /**
 465:      * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
 465:      * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
 466:      * closed/as-of query, so missing candle gaps cannot shorten history. The forward segment
 467:      * explicitly requires closed=true. No Production caller reaches this method.
 468:      */
 469:     private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
 470:         Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
 471:         Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
 472:         int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
 473:         for (String sym : symbols) {
 474:             for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
 475:                 List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 476:                         sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
 477:                 java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
 478:                 List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
 479:                         .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 480:                                 sym, interval, start, end);
 481:                 List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
 482:                 merged.addAll(prefix);
 483:                 Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
 484:                 forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
 485:                 candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
 486:                 log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
 487:                         sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
 488:             }
 489:         }
 490: 
 491:         Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
 492:         // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
 493:         // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
 494:         // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
 495:         // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().
 496:         for (TradeSignal signal : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndCandleOpenTimeBetweenOrderByCandleOpenTimeAsc(symbol, start, end)) {
 497:             ReplayDataset.SignalKey key = new ReplayDataset.SignalKey(
 498:                     signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), signal.getCandleOpenTime());
 499:             Long previous = lineage.putIfAbsent(key, signal.getId());
 500:             if (previous != null && !previous.equals(signal.getId())) {
 501:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
 502:                         + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
 503:             }
 504:         }
 505:         log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
 506:         return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
 507:     }
 508: 
 509:     private List<Candle> candles(String symbol, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
 510:         return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 511:                 symbol, interval, start, end);
 512:     }
 513: 
 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;
 517:         int size = candles.size();
 518:         for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
 519:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 520:             Candle candle = candles.get(i);
 521:             List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 522:                     symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
 523:             if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
 524:                 resolved++;
 525:             }
 526:             if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
 527:                 int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
 528:                 updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }
 532:         return resolved;
 533:     }
 534: 
 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 614:                 TradeSignal generated;
 615:                 try {
 616:                     generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
 617:                 } finally {
 618:                     profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 619:                 }
 620:                 replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
 621:                 SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
 622:                 if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
 623: 
 624:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 625:                     if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
 626:                     else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
 627:                     else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
 628: 
 629:                     if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
 630:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
 631:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
 632:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;
 633: 
 634:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 635:                     try {
 636:                         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 637:                             INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 638:                                 (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 639:                                  generated_at, latest_price, original_decision, final_decision,
 640:                                  execution_effective_decision, total_score, confidence_score, raw_confidence_score,
 641:                                  effective_confidence_score, primary_blocking_stage, detected_regime, candidate_regime,
 642:                                  confirmed_regime, regime_candidate_count, entry_authority, entry_authority_max_position_percent,
 643:                                  trend_score, volume_score, momentum_score, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
 644:                             VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, NULL)
 645:                             """,
 646:                             runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 647:                             Timestamp.from(evaluationTime), generated.getLatestPrice(),
 648:                             generated.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : generated.getOriginalDecision().name(),
 649:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 650:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 651:                             generated.getTotalScore(), generated.getConfidenceScore(), generated.getRawConfidenceScore(),
 652:                             generated.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), generated.getPrimaryBlockingStage(),
 653:                             generated.getDetectedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getDetectedRegime().name(),
 654:                             generated.getCandidateRegime() == null ? null : generated.getCandidateRegime().name(),
 655:                             generated.getConfirmedRegime() == null ? null : generated.getConfirmedRegime().name(),
 656:                             generated.getRegimeCandidateCount(), generated.getEntryAuthority(),
 657:                             generated.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), generated.getTrendScore(),
 658:                             generated.getVolumeScore(), generated.getMomentumScore());
 659:                     } finally {
 660:                         profiler.recordAnalysisTestSignalPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 661:                     }
 662: 
 663:                     // FIX-069: Persist the exact production TradeSignal shape as replay output.
 664:                     // analysis_test_signal remains for backward-compatible diagnostics, while
 665:                     // trade_signal_test is the canonical parity table used for field-by-field comparison.
 666:                     // FIX-112D: Replay lineage uses exact market-candle identity, never
 667:                     // generated_at or nearest-time matching. A legitimate Replay-only
 668:                     // signal remains NULL. This metadata lookup cannot alter Production.
 669:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 670:                     Long sourceSignalId;
 671:                     try {
 672:                         sourceSignalId = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 673:                                 ? replayDataset.sourceSignalId(
 674:                                         generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
 675:                                 : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTime(
 676:                                         generated.getSymbol(), generated.getInterval(), generated.getCandleOpenTime())
 677:                                         .map(TradeSignal::getId).orElse(null);
 678:                     } finally {
 679:                         profiler.recordLineageLookup(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 680:                     }
 681:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 682:                     try {
 683:                         persistTradeSignalTest(runId, generated, sourceSignalId, null);
 684:                     } finally {
 685:                         profiler.recordTradeSignalTestPersistence(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 686:                     }
 687:                 }
 688:             } catch (ReplayCancellationException stop) {
 689:                 throw stop;
 690:             } catch (Exception exception) {
 691:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 692:                     errors++;
 693:                     saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle, errorDetail(exception, 900));
 694:                 }
 695:                 log.warn("Regression fresh-signal generation failed: run={}, symbol={}, interval={}, candle={}",
 696:                         runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), exception);
 697:             }
 698: 
 699:             if (index % 10 == 0 && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
 700:                 int progress = 43 + (int) Math.round(30d * index / timeline.size());
 701:                 long progressStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 702:                 try {
 703:                     updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", Math.min(73, progress),
 704:                             "Generating fresh signal " + (index + 1) + "/" + timeline.size(), null);
 705:                 } finally {
 706:                     profiler.recordProgressUpdate(System.nanoTime() - progressStartedNs);
 707:                 }
 708:             }
 709:         }
 710: 
 711:         } finally {
 712:             // FIX-11M must never turn a valid Replay into ERROR if diagnostic summary logging fails.
 713:             try {
 714:                 analysisService.finishReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 715:             } catch (Exception profilingException) {
 716:                 log.warn("FIX11M_REPLAY_ANALYSIS_PROFILE_ERROR run={} message={} productionMutation=false replayDecisionMutation=false",
 717:                         runId, profilingException.getMessage());
 718:             }
 719:             profiler.logSummary(log, timeline.size());
 720:         }
 721:         return new FreshReplayStats(oneMinuteSignals, oneMinuteBuys, fiveMinuteSignals, fiveMinuteBuys,
 722:                 oneHourSignals, oneHourBuys, buys, watches, sells, strongSells, errors, generatedSignals);
 723:     }
 724: 
 725:     private void saveGenerationError(long runId, String symbol, String interval, Candle candle, String error) {
 726:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 727:                 INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 728:                     (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 729:                      generated_at, latest_price, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
 730:                 VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, ?)
 731:                 """, runId, symbol, interval, Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 732:                 Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(interval))),
 733:                 candle.getClosePrice(), error);
 734:     }
 735: 
 736:     /**
 737:      * FIX-069: write every field mapped by the TradeSignal JPA entity into trade_signal_test.
 738:      * Reflection is intentional here: whenever production TradeSignal gains another @Column,
 739:      * replay inherits it automatically instead of drifting behind a hand-maintained test schema.
 740:      */
 741:     private void persistTradeSignalTest(long runId, TradeSignal signal, Long sourceSignalId, String generationError) {
 742:         try {
 743:             java.util.List<String> columns = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 744:             java.util.List<Object> values = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 745:             columns.add("test_run_id"); values.add(runId);
 746:             // FIX-112D / Replay=Production audit lineage: this ID points only to the
 747:             // exact Production signal for the same symbol + interval + candle_open_time.
 748:             // It is additive Replay metadata and is never an execution authority.
 749:             columns.add("source_signal_id"); values.add(sourceSignalId);
 750:             columns.add("replay_generated"); values.add(1);
 751:             columns.add("generation_error"); values.add(generationError);
 752: 
 753:             for (Field field : TradeSignal.class.getDeclaredFields()) {
 754:                 if ("id".equals(field.getName()) || "createdAt".equals(field.getName())) continue;
 755:                 Column column = field.getAnnotation(Column.class);
 756:                 if (column == null) continue;
 757:                 field.setAccessible(true);
 758:                 Object value = field.get(signal);
 759:                 if (value instanceof Enum<?> e) value = e.name();
 760:                 if (value instanceof Instant instant) value = Timestamp.from(instant);
 761:                 String columnName = column.name();
 762:                 if (columnName == null || columnName.isBlank()) columnName = snakeCase(field.getName());
 763:                 columns.add(columnName);
 764:                 values.add(value);
 765:             }
 766: 
 767:             String placeholders = String.join(",", java.util.Collections.nCopies(values.size(), "?"));
 768:             jdbcTemplate.update("INSERT INTO trade_signal_test (" + String.join(",", columns) + ") VALUES (" + placeholders + ")", values.toArray());
 769:         } catch (Exception e) {
 770:             throw new IllegalStateException("Unable to persist production-shaped replay TradeSignal", e);
 771:         }
 772:     }
 773: 
 774:     private String snakeCase(String value) {
 775:         return value.replaceAll("([a-z0-9])([A-Z])", "$1_$2").toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
 776:     }

 405:             log.warn("FIX-090: regression test run {} stopped by user request", runId);
 406:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 407:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 408:                     SET status='ERROR', current_step='Stopped by user',
 409:                         error_message='Replay stopped manually by user. Test data may now be deleted.',
 410:                         failure_step='Manual stop', failure_exception=NULL, failure_root_cause=NULL,
 411:                         failure_stack_trace=NULL, heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6), completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 412:                     WHERE id=?
 413:                     """, runId);
 414:         } catch (Exception exception) {
 415:             log.error("Regression test run {} failed", runId, exception);
 416:             String failedStep = currentStep(runId);
 417:             String summary = errorDetail(exception, 1900);
 418:             Throwable root = rootCause(exception);
 419:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 420:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 421:                     SET status='ERROR', progress_percent=100, current_step='Regression test failed', heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 422:                         error_message=?, failure_step=?, failure_exception=?, failure_root_cause=?,
 423:                         failure_stack_trace=?, started_at=COALESCE(started_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 424:                         completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 425:                     WHERE id=?
 426:                     """,
 427:                     summary, failedStep, exception.getClass().getName(),
 428:                     rootCauseDetail(root, 1000), stackTrace(exception), runId);
 429:         } finally {
 430:             // FIX-11J: persist one timing snapshot after the existing Replay path has completed or
 431:             // unwound. Diagnostics persistence is deliberately best-effort: it must never replace
 432:             // the Replay's real PASSED/FAILED/ERROR outcome or swallow the original exception.
 433:             long replayTotalNs = System.nanoTime() - replayTotalStartedNs;
 434:             persistReplayTimingsSafely(runId, loadHistoricalDataNs, verifyEventResolutionNs,
 435:                     buildReplayDatasetNs, generateFreshSignalsNs, shadowExecutionNs,
 436:                     parityComparisonNs, replayTotalNs);
 437: 
 438:             // FIX-087: always release ownership, including ERROR paths, so a genuinely stopped
 439:             // replay remains recoverable later using the same run id and original replay window.
 440:             activeRunIds.remove(runId);
 441:             stopRequestedRunIds.remove(runId);
 442:         }
 443:     }

 445:     private void persistReplayTimingsSafely(long runId, Long loadHistoricalDataNs,
 446:                                             Long verifyEventResolutionNs, Long buildReplayDatasetNs,
 447:                                             Long generateFreshSignalsNs, Long shadowExecutionNs,
 448:                                             Long parityComparisonNs, long replayTotalNs) {
 449:         try {
 450:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 451:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 452:                     SET timing_load_historical_ns=?, timing_verify_event_resolution_ns=?,
 453:                         timing_build_replay_dataset_ns=?, timing_generate_fresh_signals_ns=?,
 454:                         timing_shadow_execution_ns=?, timing_parity_comparison_ns=?, timing_total_ns=?
 455:                     WHERE id=?
 456:                     """, loadHistoricalDataNs, verifyEventResolutionNs, buildReplayDatasetNs,
 457:                     generateFreshSignalsNs, shadowExecutionNs, parityComparisonNs, replayTotalNs, runId);
 458:         } catch (Exception timingPersistenceFailure) {
 459:             log.error("FIX-11J: unable to persist Replay timing diagnostics for run {}",
 460:                     runId, timingPersistenceFailure);
 461:         }
 462:     }

 464:     /**
 465:      * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
 465:      * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
 466:      * closed/as-of query, so missing candle gaps cannot shorten history. The forward segment
 467:      * explicitly requires closed=true. No Production caller reaches this method.
 468:      */
 469:     private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
 470:         Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
 471:         Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
 472:         int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
 473:         for (String sym : symbols) {
 474:             for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
 475:                 List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 476:                         sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
 477:                 java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
 478:                 List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
 479:                         .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 480:                                 sym, interval, start, end);
 481:                 List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
 482:                 merged.addAll(prefix);
 483:                 Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
 484:                 forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
 485:                 candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
 486:                 log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
 487:                         sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
 488:             }
 489:         }
 490: 
 491:         Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
 492:         // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
 493:         // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
 494:         // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
 495:         // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().
 496:         for (TradeSignal signal : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndCandleOpenTimeBetweenOrderByCandleOpenTimeAsc(symbol, start, end)) {
 497:             ReplayDataset.SignalKey key = new ReplayDataset.SignalKey(
 498:                     signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), signal.getCandleOpenTime());
 499:             Long previous = lineage.putIfAbsent(key, signal.getId());
 500:             if (previous != null && !previous.equals(signal.getId())) {
 501:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
 502:                         + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
 503:             }
 504:         }
 505:         log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
 506:         return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
 507:     }

 510:         return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(

 316:                 int effectivePercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100,
 317:                         (int)Math.round(atrPercent * decision.positionPercent() / 100.0)));
 318:                 WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
 319:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
 320:                         effectivePercent, 0, true, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),
 321:                         replayExecutedNewPositions, fresh.price());
 322:                 BigDecimal budget = sizing.spend();
 323:                 if (sizing.allowed()) {
 324:                     BigDecimal qty = sizing.quantity();
 325:                     effectivePercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
 326:                     long positionId = openPosition(runId, symbol, executionSignal, fresh, qty, budget, effectivePercent);
 327:                     persistBuy(runId, symbol, executionSignal, fresh, qty, budget, effectivePercent, decision);
 328:                     executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(executionSignal, decision);
 329:                     cash = cash.subtract(budget, MC);
 330:                     replayExecutedNewPositions++;
 331:                     // Keep the complete immutable BUY thesis needed by production continuation.
 332:                     // Replay must not drop entry structure and silently evaluate a different exit policy.
 333:                     open = new ShadowPosition(positionId, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), fresh.price(), qty, budget,
 334:                             effectivePercent, executionSignal.getStopLoss(), executionSignal.getTakeProfit(), fresh.price(), false, null,
 335:                             ProfitLockState.INACTIVE, null,
 336:                             executionSignal.getTotalScore(), executionSignal.getConfidenceScore(), executionSignal.getTrendScore(), executionSignal.getTrendStructureScore(),
 337:                             executionSignal.getMomentumScore(), executionSignal.getVolumeScore(),
 338:                             NearTpState.INACTIVE, null, 0, null, false, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 339:                     // FIX-112A: only an actually opened shadow position consumes the bullish entry.
 340:                     replayScope.markEntryConsumed(executionSignal.getId());
 341:                 }
 342:             } else if (open != null && decision.allowed()) {
 343:                 // FIX-056: progressive Replay adds use the same fresh execution price and
 344:                 // execution-time Entry Quality revalidation as Production.
 345:                 ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh = executionPriceAuthorityService
 346:                         .resolve(symbol, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 347:                 if (fresh == null) continue;
 348:                 decision = executionIntelligenceService.revalidateAtExecutionPrice(signal, decision, fresh.price());
 349:                 if (!decision.allowed()) continue;
 350:                 int addPercent = Math.max(1, Math.min(100 - open.positionPercent(), decision.positionPercent()));
 351:                 WalletExecutionSizingPolicy.Plan sizing = executionSizingPolicy.plan(
 352:                         cash, replayWalletSettings.getMinimumUsdtReserve(), replayDailyBudget,
 353:                         addPercent, open.positionPercent(), false, replayWalletSettings.getMaximumDailyNewPositions(),
 354:                         replayExecutedNewPositions, fresh.price());
 355:                 BigDecimal budget = sizing.spend();
 356:                 if (sizing.allowed()) {
 357:                     addPercent = sizing.normalizedPositionPercent();
 358:                     BigDecimal addedQty = sizing.quantity();
 359:                     persistBuy(runId, symbol, signal, fresh, addedQty, budget, addPercent, decision);
 360:                     executionIntelligenceService.markExecuted(signal, decision);
 361:                     // FIX-112A: a progressive add consumes only its own triggering signal.
 362:                     replayScope.markEntryConsumed(signal.getId());
 363:                     cash = cash.subtract(budget, MC);
 364:                     open = addToPosition(runId, open, signal, addedQty, budget, addPercent);
 365:                 }
 366:             }
 367:         }
 368:         }
 369: 
 370:         BigDecimal finalWallet = cash;
 371:         if (open != null && !timeline.isEmpty()) {
 372:             BigDecimal mark = timeline.get(timeline.size() - 1).getLatestPrice();
 373:             if (mark != null) finalWallet = finalWallet.add(mark.multiply(open.quantity(), MC), MC);
 374:         }
 375: 
 376:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 377:                 UPDATE analysis_test_run
 378:                 SET simulated_trade_count=?, simulated_win_count=?, simulated_loss_count=?,
 379:                     simulated_realized_pnl=?, simulated_final_wallet=?
 380:                 WHERE id=?
 381:                 """, trades, wins, losses, realized, finalWallet, runId);
 382:         defensiveRiskReductionObserver.logRunSummary(runId, symbol);
 383:         oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.finishRun(runId, symbol, executionEnd);
 384:         // FIX-109: expose whether this run had the persisted live-price stream required
 385:         // for tick-exact production ordering. Older windows remain explicitly labelled
 386:         // as fallback instead of being mistaken for exact parity.
 387:         return new ReplayStats(trades, wins, losses, realized, finalWallet,
 388:                 exactPriceReplay ? "EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY" : "SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK",
 389:                 replayLogicMode);
 390:     }
