 392:     /**
 393:      * FIX-052: mechanical position protection evaluated from the exact Production
 394:      * 1m live-price observation stream. The order mirrors LivePositionProtectionService:
 395:      * TAKE_PROFIT -> STOP_LOSS -> PROFIT_LOCK -> normal HTF exit. The newly closed
 396:      * 1m signal is intentionally not visible yet; BinanceKlineService calls onPrice()
 397:      * before publishing the candle-close event in Production.
 398:      */
 399:     private LivePriceEvaluation evaluateLivePrice(long runId, String symbol, ShadowPosition p,
 400:                                                    MarketPriceEventService.PriceEvent event,
 401:                                                    TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
 402:         BigDecimal price = event.price();
 403:         ShadowPosition current = p;
 404: 
 405:         if (current.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(current.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 406:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 407:                     oneMinute, five, one,
 408:                     current.entryTrend(), current.entryStructure(), current.entryMomentum(), current.entryVolume(),
 409:                     current.entryConfidence(), current.entryScore());
 410:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 411:                 BigDecimal distance = current.takeProfit().subtract(current.entryPrice());
 412:                 BigDecimal oldTarget = current.takeProfit();
 413:                 BigDecimal newTarget = oldTarget.add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 414:                 ProfitLockState beforeState = current.profitLockState();
 415:                 current = rebaseForExtendedTarget(current, newTarget, price, event.observedAt()).withTakeProfit(newTarget);
 416:                 BigDecimal fix118NewProgress = progressPercent(current.entryPrice(), newTarget, current.highest());
 417:                 log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][TP_EXTENSION] runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, observedAt={}, marketPrice={}, " +
 418:                                 "entry={}, oldTp={}, newTp={}, highest={}, profitLockActive={}, profitLockPrice={}, previousState={}, state={}, " +
 419:                                 "newGeometryProgressPct={}",
 420:                         runId, current.positionId(), symbol, event.observedAt(), price, current.entryPrice(), oldTarget, newTarget,
 421:                         current.highest(), current.profitLockActive(), current.profitLockPrice(), beforeState, current.profitLockState(), fix118NewProgress);
 422:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, current.positionId());
 423:                 persistProfitLockState(current);
 424:                 persistNearTpState(current);
 425:                 persistManagementAtPrice(runId, symbol, event, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", oldTarget, newTarget, current, continuation.explanation());
 426:                 return new LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision.hold(), current);
 427:             }
 428:             // FIX-11Q: baseline continuation has already failed. Only now may the replay-only
 429:             // counterfactual evaluate the one-candle grace. Existing PASS/TP-extension behavior above
 430:             // always wins first and is never intercepted by this experiment.
 431:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
 432:                     runId, symbol, current.positionId(), event.observedAt(), event.price(),
 433:                     current.entryTime(), current.entryPrice(), current.quantity(), current.cost(),
 434:                     current.stopLoss(), current.takeProfit(), current.highest(),
 435:                     current.profitLockActive(), current.profitLockPrice(),
 436:                     current.entryScore(), current.entryConfidence(), current.entryTrend(), current.entryStructure(),
 437:                     current.entryMomentum(), current.entryVolume(), oneMinute, five, one, continuation.explanation());
 438:             persistManagementAtPrice(runId, symbol, event, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", current.takeProfit(), current.takeProfit(), current, continuation.explanation());
 439:             return new LivePriceEvaluation(new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation()), current);
 440:         }
 441: 
 442:         // Production stop-loss has priority over profit-lock evaluation.
 443:         if (current.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(current.stopLoss()) <= 0) {
 444:             return new LivePriceEvaluation(new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS",
 445:                     "Live price " + price + " reached stop loss " + current.stopLoss()), current);
 446:         }
 447: 
 448:         ShadowPosition updated = profitLockState(current, price);
 449:         if (!equalsNullable(current.highest(), updated.highest())
 450:                 || current.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()
 451:                 || !equalsNullable(current.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice())
 452:                 || current.profitLockState() != updated.profitLockState()
 453:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(current.profitLockRebaseStartedAt(), updated.profitLockRebaseStartedAt())) {
 454:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET highest_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_active=?, profit_lock_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_state=?, profit_lock_rebase_started_at=? WHERE id=?",
 455:                     updated.highest(), updated.profitLockActive(), updated.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockState().name(),
 456:                     updated.profitLockRebaseStartedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(updated.profitLockRebaseStartedAt()), updated.positionId());
 457:         }
 458:         if (updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE
 459:                 || updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 460:                 || !equalsNullable(current.highest(), updated.highest())
 900: 
 901:     private long openPosition(long runId,String symbol,TradeSignal s,ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.ExecutionPrice fresh,BigDecimal qty,BigDecimal budget,int pct){
 902:         org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder kh=new org.springframework.jdbc.support.GeneratedKeyHolder();
 903:         jdbcTemplate.update(c->{var ps=c.prepareStatement("""
 904:             INSERT INTO wallet_position_test
 905:             (test_run_id,symbol,status,entry_time,entry_price,quantity,total_cost_usdt,position_percent,stop_loss_usdt,take_profit_usdt,highest_price_usdt)
 906:             VALUES (?,?,'OPEN',?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)""",java.sql.Statement.RETURN_GENERATED_KEYS);
 907:             ps.setLong(1,runId);ps.setString(2,symbol);ps.setTimestamp(3,Timestamp.from(fresh.observedAt()));ps.setBigDecimal(4,fresh.price());ps.setBigDecimal(5,qty);ps.setBigDecimal(6,budget);ps.setInt(7,pct);ps.setBigDecimal(8,s.getStopLoss());ps.setBigDecimal(9,s.getTakeProfit());ps.setBigDecimal(10,fresh.price());return ps;},kh);
 908:         if (kh.getKey() == null) throw new IllegalStateException("Could not create shadow position");
 909:         return kh.getKey().longValue();
 910:     }

1009:     private BigDecimal nvl(BigDecimal value, BigDecimal fallback) { return value == null ? fallback : value; }

1014:                 + "%, initial=" + nvl(s.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)).stripTrailingZeros().toPlainString()

1018:     private BigDecimal totalPositionPnlPercent(ShadowPosition position, BigDecimal remainingLegPnl) {
1019:         BigDecimal totalPnl = position.partialRealizedPnl().add(remainingLegPnl, MC);
1020:         BigDecimal totalInvestedCost = position.cost().add(position.partialHarvestCostBasis(), MC);
1021:         return totalInvestedCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO
1022:                 : totalPnl.divide(totalInvestedCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
1023:     }

1025:     private BigDecimal percentage(BigDecimal entry,BigDecimal exit){return exit.subtract(entry).multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).divide(entry,8,RoundingMode.HALF_UP);}
1026:     private boolean bullish(SignalDecision d){return d==SignalDecision.BUY||d==SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;}
1027:     private boolean bearish(SignalDecision d){return d==SignalDecision.SELL||d==SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;}
1028:     private String name(SignalDecision d){return d==null?null:d.name();}
1029:     private int intervalOrder(String i){return "1h".equals(i)?0:"5m".equals(i)?1:2;}
1030:     private boolean equalsNullable(BigDecimal a,BigDecimal b){return a==null?b==null:b!=null&&a.compareTo(b)==0;}

1032:     public record ReplayStats(int trades, int wins, int losses, BigDecimal realizedPnl, BigDecimal finalWallet,
1033:                               String priceReplayMode, String logicMode) {}
1034:     private record FallbackNearTpEvaluation(ShadowPosition position, BigDecimal partialProceeds,
1035:                                             BigDecimal partialRealizedPnl) {}
1036:     private record LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision decision, ShadowPosition position,
1037:                                        BigDecimal partialProceeds, BigDecimal partialRealizedPnl) {
1038:         LivePriceEvaluation(ExitDecision decision, ShadowPosition position) {
1039:             this(decision, position, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
1040:         }
1041:     }
1042:     private record ExitDecision(boolean exit,String reason,String explanation,BigDecimal newTakeProfit){
1043:         ExitDecision(boolean exit,String reason,String explanation){this(exit,reason,explanation,null);}
1044:         static ExitDecision hold(){return new ExitDecision(false,"HOLD","Position remains open.",null);}
1045:     }
1046:     private record ShadowPosition(long positionId, Instant entryTime, BigDecimal entryPrice, BigDecimal quantity,
1047:                                   BigDecimal cost, int positionPercent, BigDecimal stopLoss, BigDecimal takeProfit,
1048:                                   BigDecimal highest, boolean profitLockActive, BigDecimal profitLockPrice,
1049:                                   ProfitLockState profitLockState, Instant profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1050:                                   int entryScore, int entryConfidence, int entryTrend, int entryStructure,
1051:                                   int entryMomentum, int entryVolume, NearTpState nearTpState, BigDecimal nearTpBestPrice,
1052:                                   int nearTpBearishStreak, Long nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId, boolean nearTpHarvestUsed,
1053:                                   BigDecimal nearTpHarvestedQuantity, BigDecimal partialRealizedPnl,
1054:                                   BigDecimal partialHarvestCostBasis) {
1055:         ShadowPosition withLock(BigDecimal h, boolean a, BigDecimal l, ProfitLockState state, Instant rebaseStartedAt) {
1056:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, positionPercent, stopLoss, takeProfit,
1057:                     h, a, l, state, rebaseStartedAt,
1058:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1059:                     nearTpState, nearTpBestPrice, nearTpBearishStreak, nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId, nearTpHarvestUsed,
1060:                     nearTpHarvestedQuantity, partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1061:         }
1062:         ShadowPosition withTakeProfit(BigDecimal tp) {
1063:             // FIX-117 Replay parity: TP extension preserves already-earned Near-TP protection
1064:             // exactly like Production. REJECTION_DETECTED keeps its state/best price but clears
1065:             // bearish persistence accumulated under the old geometry. INACTIVE and
1066:             // FAILURE_CONFIRMED keep the pre-FIX-117 reset behavior. Harvested state is terminal.
1067:             NearTpState state = nearTpState;
1068:             BigDecimal bestPrice = nearTpBestPrice;
1069:             int bearishStreak = nearTpBearishStreak;
1070:             Long lastOneMinuteSignalId = nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId;
1071: 
1072:             if (!nearTpHarvestUsed) {
1073:                 switch (nearTpState) {
1074:                     case INACTIVE, NEAR_TP_FAILURE_CONFIRMED -> {
1075:                         state = NearTpState.INACTIVE;
1076:                         bestPrice = null;
1077:                         bearishStreak = 0;
1078:                         lastOneMinuteSignalId = null;
1079:                     }
1080:                     case NEAR_TP_ARMED -> { }
1081:                     case NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED -> {
1082:                         bearishStreak = 0;
1083:                         lastOneMinuteSignalId = null;
1084:                     }
1085:                     case NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED -> { }
1086:                 }
1087:             }
1088: 
1089:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, positionPercent, stopLoss, tp,
1090:                     highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice, profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1091:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1092:                     state, bestPrice, bearishStreak, lastOneMinuteSignalId, nearTpHarvestUsed,
1093:                     nearTpHarvestedQuantity, partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1094:         }
1095:         ShadowPosition withAdd(BigDecimal newEntry, BigDecimal newQuantity, BigDecimal newCost, int newPercent, BigDecimal newStop, BigDecimal newTakeProfit) {
1096:             NearTpState state = nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpState : NearTpState.INACTIVE;
1097:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit,
1098:                     highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice, profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1099:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1100:                     state, nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpBestPrice : null, nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpBearishStreak : 0,
1101:                     nearTpHarvestUsed ? nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId : null, nearTpHarvestUsed, nearTpHarvestedQuantity,
1102:                     partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1103:         }
1104:         ShadowPosition withNearTp(NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state) {
1105:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity, cost, positionPercent, stopLoss, takeProfit,
1106:                     highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice, profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1107:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1108:                     state.nearTpState(), state.bestPrice(), state.consecutiveBearishOneMinute(), state.lastEvaluatedOneMinuteSignalId(),
1109:                     state.harvestUsed(), nearTpHarvestedQuantity, partialRealizedPnl, partialHarvestCostBasis);
1110:         }
1111:         ShadowPosition withPartialHarvest(BigDecimal soldQty, BigDecimal soldCost, BigDecimal realizedPnl) {
1112:             return new ShadowPosition(positionId, entryTime, entryPrice, quantity.subtract(soldQty, MC), cost.subtract(soldCost, MC),
1113:                     positionPercent, stopLoss, takeProfit, highest, profitLockActive, profitLockPrice,
1114:                     profitLockState, profitLockRebaseStartedAt,
1115:                     entryScore, entryConfidence, entryTrend, entryStructure, entryMomentum, entryVolume,
1116:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED, nearTpBestPrice, nearTpBearishStreak, nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId, true,
1117:                     nearTpHarvestedQuantity.add(soldQty, MC), partialRealizedPnl.add(realizedPnl, MC),
1118:                     partialHarvestCostBasis.add(soldCost, MC));
1119:         }
1120:         boolean sameNearTp(ShadowPosition other) {
1121:             return nearTpState == other.nearTpState
1122:                     && equalsDecimal(nearTpBestPrice, other.nearTpBestPrice)
1123:                     && nearTpBearishStreak == other.nearTpBearishStreak
1124:                     && java.util.Objects.equals(nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId, other.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId)
1125:                     && nearTpHarvestUsed == other.nearTpHarvestUsed;
1126:         }
1127:         private boolean equalsDecimal(BigDecimal a, BigDecimal b) {
1128:             return a == null ? b == null : b != null && a.compareTo(b) == 0;
1129:         }

 562:     private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
 563:         BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
 564:         if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
 565: 
 566:         // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
 567:         // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
 568:         // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
 569:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
 570:         if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 571:             // Exact production TP-continuation call, including immutable entry structure.
 572:             // FIX-011 regression relies on Replay and live using this same shared policy.
 573:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 574:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 575:                     p.entryTrend(), p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(),
 576:                     p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());
 577:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 578:                 BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
 579:                 BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 580:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
 581:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
 582:                 return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
 583:             }
 584:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
 585:                     runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 586:                     p.entryTime(), p.entryPrice(), p.quantity(), p.cost(), p.stopLoss(), p.takeProfit(), p.highest(),
 587:                     p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.entryScore(), p.entryConfidence(), p.entryTrend(),
 588:                     p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(), oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 589:                     continuation.explanation());
 590:             persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), p, continuation.explanation());
 591:             return new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation());
 592:         }
 593:         // FIX-052 fallback ordering also mirrors Production: STOP_LOSS is absolute and
 594:         // is evaluated before profit-lock. Exact V64 runs normally take this path from
 595:         // persisted live-price events rather than from signal-carried prices.
 596:         if (authoritativePrice && p.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(p.stopLoss()) <= 0)
 597:             return new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the stored stop loss.");
 598: 
 599:         ShadowPosition updated = authoritativePrice ? profitLockState(p, price) : p;
 600:         if (authoritativePrice && (updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE
 601:                 || updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 602:                 || p.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()
 603:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice())
 604:                 || p.profitLockState() != updated.profitLockState())) {
 605:             BigDecimal fix118Progress = updated.takeProfit() == null || updated.takeProfit().compareTo(updated.entryPrice()) <= 0
 606:                     ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 607:                     : updated.highest().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC)
 608:                             .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 609:                             .divide(updated.takeProfit().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 610:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_PATH] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, " +
 611:                             "entry={}, target={}, highest={}, previousActive={}, active={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousLock={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, triggered={}",
 612:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 613:                     updated.entryPrice(), updated.takeProfit(), updated.highest(), p.profitLockActive(), updated.profitLockActive(),
 614:                     p.profitLockState(), updated.profitLockState(), p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice(), fix118Progress,
 615:                     updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 616:                             && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0);
 617:         }
 618:         BigDecimal minimumProfitableExit = p.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
 619:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 620:                 && price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 621:             String floorReason = "TP-extension rebase safety floor was breached at " + minimumProfitableExit + ". "
 622:                     + profitLockConfigText();
 623:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, floor={}",
 624:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price, minimumProfitableExit);
 625:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 626:             return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 627:         }
 628:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 629:                 && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
 630:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, lock={}, target={}",
 631:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 632:                     updated.profitLockPrice(), updated.takeProfit());
 633:             if (price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 634:                 String floorReason = "Profit-lock hard floor was breached; protected profit can no longer be preserved. "
 635:                         + profitLockConfigText();
 636:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 637:                 return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 638:             }
 639:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(
 640:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 641:             if (!lockDecision.exit()) {
 642:                 String holdReason = lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 643:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, holdReason);
 644:                 return ExitDecision.hold();
 645:             }
 646:             String lockReason = "Price retraced to the protected profit level after a profitable advance. "
 647:                     + lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 648:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, lockReason);
 649:             return new ExitDecision(true, lockDecision.code(), lockReason);
 650:         }
 650:         }
 651:         // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the
 652:         // 1m signal TradeExecutionValidationService. Replay executes both shared production
 653:         // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.
 654:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(
 655:                 oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 656:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 657:             return new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation());
 658:         }
 659:         if ("1m".equals(s.getInterval()) && bearish(s.getDecision())) {
 660:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(s);
 661:             if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
 662:                 return new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
 663:             }
 664:         }
 665:         return ExitDecision.hold();
 666:     }
 667: 
 668:     private FallbackNearTpEvaluation evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(long runId, ShadowPosition current,
 669:                                                                     TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal oneMinute,
 670:                                                                     TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 671:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 672:                 current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
 673:                 current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
 674:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 675:                 state, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt(),
 676:                 oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 677:         ShadowPosition updated = current.withNearTp(evaluation.state());
 678:         if (!updated.sameNearTp(current)) persistNearTpState(updated);
 679:         if (evaluation.transition()) {
 680:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
 681:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), updated.nearTpState(),
 682:                     signal.getLatestPrice(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), updated.nearTpBearishStreak(), evaluation.explanation());
 683:         }
 684:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 685: 
 686:         BigDecimal soldQty = updated.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
 687:         BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
 688:                 binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, signal.getLatestPrice());
 689:         if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
 690:             updated = updated.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 691:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, updated.nearTpBestPrice(),
 692:                     0, updated.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
 693:             persistNearTpState(updated);
 694:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
 695:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
 696:                     minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
 697:             return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 698:         }
 699:         BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
 700:         BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 701:         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 702:         BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 703:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
 704:         updated = updated.withPartialHarvest(soldQty, soldCost, pnl);
 705:         persistNearTpPartialHarvestAtSignal(runId, signal, updated, soldQty, proceeds, pnl, pnlPct, evaluation.explanation());
 706:         log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_PARTIAL_HARVESTED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, soldQty={}, remainingQty={}, price={}, partialPnl={}",
 707:                 runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, updated.quantity(), signal.getLatestPrice(), pnl);
 708:         return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, proceeds, pnl);
 709:     }

 711:     private ShadowPosition updateProfitLock(ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s) {
 712:         if (s.getLatestPrice() == null || !priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime())) return p;
 713:         ShadowPosition n = profitLockState(p, s.getLatestPrice());
 714:         if (!equalsNullable(p.highest(), n.highest()) || p.profitLockActive() != n.profitLockActive()
 715:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockPrice())
 716:                 || p.profitLockState() != n.profitLockState()
 717:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt(), n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt())) {
 718:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET highest_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_active=?, profit_lock_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_state=?, profit_lock_rebase_started_at=? WHERE id=?",
 719:                     n.highest(), n.profitLockActive(), n.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockState().name(),
 720:                     n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt()), p.positionId());
 721:         }
 722:         return n;
 723:     }
 724: 
 725:     private ShadowPosition profitLockState(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 726:         WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 727:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(settings.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 728:         BigDecimal highest = p.highest() == null ? price : p.highest().max(price);
 729:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), highest);
 730: 
 731:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 732:                 && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 733:             // Replay parity with Production FIX-118: preserve the historical lock/legacy flag,
 734:             // but keep it non-executable until the new TP geometry earns activation again.
 735:             return p.withLock(highest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 736:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 737:         }
 738: 
 739:         // FIX-118 parity: when REBASE earns activation, rebuild the lock from the current
 740:         // TP geometry instead of retaining the superseded pre-extension lock.
 741:         boolean freshGeometryActivation = p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 742:         ProfitLockPolicy.State state = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 743:                 p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), price, highest,
 744:                 freshGeometryActivation ? false : (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || p.profitLockActive()),
 745:                 freshGeometryActivation ? null : p.profitLockPrice(),
 746:                 settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 747:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 748:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 749:         ProfitLockState nextState = state.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 750:         return p.withLock(state.highestPrice(), state.active(), state.lockPrice(), nextState, null);
 751:     }
 752: 
 753:     private ShadowPosition rebaseForExtendedTarget(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant at) {
 754:         if (p == null || newTarget == null) return p;
 755:         BigDecimal oldTarget = p.takeProfit();
 756:         BigDecimal oldLock = p.profitLockPrice();
 757:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(walletSettings().getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 758:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = p.highest() == null ? p.entryPrice() : p.highest();
 759:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 760:         ShadowPosition withExtensionHigh = p.withLock(extensionHighest, p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(),
 761:                 p.profitLockState(), p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 762:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 763:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 764:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 765:                 ShadowPosition rebasing = withExtensionHigh.withLock(extensionHighest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 766:                         at == null ? Instant.now() : at);
 767:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 768:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 769:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), rebasing.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 770:                         extensionHighest, progress, oldLock, rebasing.profitLockPrice(), activation);
 771:                 return rebasing;
 772:             }
 773:             // FIX-120 Production/Replay parity: the old executable lock belongs to the
 774:             // superseded TP geometry. If the extended winner still qualifies, establish a
 775:             // fresh geometry baseline. Raw extensionPrice is current; extensionHighest is the
 776:             // historical-best seed; previousActive/previousLock are deliberately discarded.
 777:             WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 778:             BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
 779:             ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 780:                     p.entryPrice(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
 781:                     false, null, settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 782:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 783:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 784:             if (rebased.active()) {
 785:                 ShadowPosition active = withExtensionHigh.withLock(rebased.highestPrice(), true, rebased.lockPrice(), ProfitLockState.ACTIVE, null);
 786:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 787:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 788:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), active.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 789:                         rebased.highestPrice(), rebased.progressPercent(), oldLock, rebased.lockPrice(), activation);
 790:                 return active;
 791:             }
 792:             ShadowPosition rebasing = withExtensionHigh.withLock(rebased.highestPrice(), true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 793:                     at == null ? Instant.now() : at);
 794:             log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 795:                             "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 796:                     p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), rebasing.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 797:                     rebased.highestPrice(), rebased.progressPercent(), oldLock, rebasing.profitLockPrice(), activation);
 798:             return rebasing;
 799:         }
 800:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 801:             return withExtensionHigh.withLock(extensionHighest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 802:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 803:         }
 804:         return withExtensionHigh;
 805:     }
 806: 
 807:     private BigDecimal progressPercent(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal target, BigDecimal highest) {
 808:         if (entry == null || target == null || highest == null || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
 809:         BigDecimal favorable = highest.subtract(entry);
 810:         if (favorable.signum() < 0) favorable = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 811:         return favorable.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 812:                 .divide(target.subtract(entry, MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 813:     }

 817:                 p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.profitLockState().name(),
 818:                 p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt()), p.positionId());
 819:     }

 841:     private String replayStage(int allocationPercent) {
 842:         if (allocationPercent <= 0) return "NONE";
 843:         if (allocationPercent < 50) return "SCOUT_ENTRY";
 844:         if (allocationPercent < 100) return "CONFIRMATION_ADD";
 845:         return "TREND_ADD";
 846:     }

 848:     private ShadowPosition addToPosition(long runId, ShadowPosition open, TradeSignal signal,
 849:                                          BigDecimal addedQty, BigDecimal addedCost, int addPercent) {
 850:         BigDecimal newQuantity = open.quantity().add(addedQty, MC);
 851:         BigDecimal newCost = open.cost().add(addedCost, MC);
 852:         BigDecimal newEntry = newCost.divide(newQuantity, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 853:         BigDecimal newStop = open.stopLoss();
 854:         if (signal.getStopLoss() != null && (newStop == null || signal.getStopLoss().compareTo(newStop) > 0)) {
 855:             newStop = signal.getStopLoss();
 856:         }
 857:         BigDecimal newTakeProfit = open.takeProfit();
 858:         if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null && (newTakeProfit == null || signal.getTakeProfit().compareTo(newTakeProfit) > 0)) {
 859:             newTakeProfit = signal.getTakeProfit();
 860:         }
 861:         int newPercent = Math.min(100, open.positionPercent() + addPercent);
 862:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 863:                 UPDATE wallet_position_test
 864:                 SET entry_price=?, quantity=?, total_cost_usdt=?, position_percent=?,
 865:                     stop_loss_usdt=?, take_profit_usdt=?
 866:                 WHERE id=? AND test_run_id=?
 867:                 """, newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit,
 868:                 open.positionId(), runId);
 869:         ShadowPosition updated = open.withAdd(newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit);
 870:         persistNearTpState(updated);
 871:         return updated;
 872:     }

   1: package com.crypto.wallet.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.client.binance.BinanceMarketDataClient;
   4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   5: import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   7: 
   8: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   9: import java.math.MathContext;
  10: import java.util.Locale;
  11: 
  12: /**
  13:  * FIX-11T minimum-executable-order guard shared by Production Paper and Replay.
  14:  *
  15:  * The project intentionally models Paper/Replay as real Binance trading with only the
  16:  * final connector missing.  Therefore a Near-TP partial harvest that Binance would reject
  17:  * for being below the symbol's minimum notional is not simulated.  No alternative exit
  18:  * size is invented: the position simply continues through its existing management logic.
  19:  *
  20:  * This class deliberately checks only the agreed minimum executable notional boundary.
  21:  * It does not add a new Near-TP-specific step-size/precision model.
  22:  */
  23: @Component
  24: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  25: @Slf4j
  26: public class BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy {
  27:     private static final MathContext MC = MathContext.DECIMAL64;
  28: 
  29:     private final BinanceMarketDataClient binanceMarketDataClient;
  30:     public Evaluation evaluate(String symbol, BigDecimal quantity, BigDecimal price) {
  31:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank() || quantity == null || quantity.signum() <= 0
  32:                 || price == null || price.signum() <= 0) {
  33:             return new Evaluation(false, "INVALID_ORDER_INPUT", BigDecimal.ZERO, null);
  34:         }
  35:         String pair = symbol.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  36:         BigDecimal notional = quantity.multiply(price, MC);
  37:         BigDecimal minimum;
  38:         try {
  39:             minimum = binanceMarketDataClient.getMinimumExecutableNotional(pair);
  40:         } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
  41:             // Fail safe. Missing exchange constraints must never be converted into permission
  42:             // to create an order that real Binance might reject.
  43:             log.warn("FIX-11T Binance minimum executable amount unavailable: symbol={}, requestedNotional={}, error={}",
  44:                     pair, notional, ex.getMessage());
  45:             return new Evaluation(false, "BINANCE_MINIMUM_UNAVAILABLE", notional, null);
  46:         }
  47:         if (notional.compareTo(minimum) < 0) {
  48:             return new Evaluation(false, "BELOW_BINANCE_MINIMUM", notional, minimum);
  49:         }
  50:         return new Evaluation(true, "EXECUTABLE", notional, minimum);
  51:     }
  52: 
  53:     public record Evaluation(boolean executable, String code, BigDecimal requestedNotional, BigDecimal minimumNotional) {
  54:     }
  55: }
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.market.service.MarketPriceEventService;
   4: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   6: 
   7: import java.math.BigDecimal;
   8: import java.time.Duration;
   9: import java.time.Instant;
  10: import java.util.Optional;
  11: import java.util.Objects;
  12: 
  13: /**
  14:  * FIX-056 fresh execution-price authority.
  15:  *
  16:  * <p>TradeSignal.latestPrice is immutable decision-time evidence and must never be
  17:  * treated as an execution fill. Production resolves the newest canonical Binance 1m
  18:  * live-price event recorded by FIX-052. Administration Replay resolves the newest
  19:  * replay price event already consumed by ExecutionReplayScope. This gives both paths
  20:  * one price authority while preserving the original signal snapshot for audit.</p>
  21:  */
  22: @Service
  23: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  24: public class ExecutionPriceAuthorityService {
  25:     private static final Duration MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE = Duration.ofSeconds(15);
  26: 
  27:     private final MarketPriceEventService marketPriceEventService;
  28:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  29: 
  30:     public Optional<ExecutionPrice> resolve(String symbol, Instant referenceTime) {
  31:         // FIX-109 / Replay parity: wall-clock fallback is forbidden. Every caller must
  32:         // supply the evaluation clock explicitly so historical Replay can never silently
  33:         // compare an old market event with the machine clock used by Production today.
  34:         Instant reference = Objects.requireNonNull(referenceTime,
  35:                 "Execution price reference time is required");
  36: 
  37:         if (replayScope.active()) {
  38:             return replayScope.latestPrice()
  39:                     .filter(p -> symbol != null && symbol.equalsIgnoreCase(p.symbol()))
  40:                     .filter(p -> !p.observedAt().isAfter(reference))
  41:                     .filter(p -> !Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).isNegative())
  42:                     .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
  43:                     .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), "REPLAY_MARKET_PRICE_EVENT"));
  44:         }
  45: 
  46:         return marketPriceEventService.findLatestAtOrBefore(symbol, reference)
  47:                 .filter(p -> Duration.between(p.observedAt(), reference).compareTo(MAX_EXECUTION_PRICE_AGE) <= 0)
  48:                 .map(p -> new ExecutionPrice(p.price(), p.observedAt(), p.source()));
  49:     }
  50: 
  51:     public record ExecutionPrice(BigDecimal price, Instant observedAt, String source) {}
  52: }
   1: package com.crypto.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.MarketRegime;
   4: import com.crypto.dto.MarketRegimeAssessment;
   5: import com.crypto.execution.service.ExecutionReplayScope;
   6: import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
   7: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   8: 
   9: import java.time.Instant;
  10: import java.util.HashMap;
  11: import java.util.List;
  12: import java.util.Map;
  13: 
  14: /**
  15:  * FIX-091 / Fix 4 + FIX-109: Experimental-Replay-only regime persistence state machine.
  16:  * Production behavior is intentionally untouched until the parity gate is passed.
  17:  * State is keyed by replay run + symbol + interval and advances only on candle timestamps.
  18:  */
  19: @Service
  20: public class RegimeStateService {
  21:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  22:     private final int requiredCandles;
  23:     // Run id is part of the key so concurrent Replay/Test workers cannot reset or contaminate
  24:     // one another. The map contains only Replay state; Production never reads it.
  25:     private final Map<String, RegimeState> replayState = new HashMap<>();
  26: 
  27:     public RegimeStateService(ExecutionReplayScope replayScope,
  28:                               @Value("${analysis.regime.persistence-candles:3}") int requiredCandles) {
  29:         this.replayScope = replayScope;
  30:         this.requiredCandles = Math.max(2, requiredCandles);
  31:     }
  32: 
  33:     public synchronized Decision apply(String symbol, String interval, Instant candleTime,
  34:                                        MarketRegimeAssessment detectedAssessment) {
  35:         if (detectedAssessment == null || !replayScope.active() || !replayScope.experimental()) {
  36:             MarketRegime regime = detectedAssessment == null ? MarketRegime.UNKNOWN : detectedAssessment.regime();
  37:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, regime, null, regime, 0, false);
  38:         }
  39: 
  40:         long runId = replayScope.runId();
  41:         String key = runId + "|" + symbol + "|" + interval;
  42:         RegimeState state = replayState.get(key);
  43:         MarketRegime detected = detectedAssessment.regime();
  44:         if (state == null) {
  45:             state = new RegimeState(detected, null, 0, candleTime);
  46:             replayState.put(key, state);
  47:             return new Decision(detectedAssessment, detected, null, detected, 0, false);
  48:         }
  49: 
  50:         MarketRegime confirmed = state.confirmed;
  51:         MarketRegime candidate = state.candidate;
  52:         int count = state.candidateCount;
  53:         boolean promoted = false;
  54: 
  55:         if (detected == confirmed) {
  56:             candidate = null;
  57:             count = 0;
  58:         } else if (detected == candidate) {
  59:             count++;
  60:         } else {
  61:             candidate = detected;
  62:             count = 1;
  63:         }
  64: 
  65:         if (candidate != null && count >= requiredCandles) {
  66:             confirmed = candidate;
  67:             candidate = null;
  68:             count = 0;
  69:             promoted = true;
  70:         }
  71:         replayState.put(key, new RegimeState(confirmed, candidate, count, candleTime));
  72: 
  73:         MarketRegimeAssessment effective = detected == confirmed
  74:                 ? detectedAssessment
  75:                 : new MarketRegimeAssessment(confirmed, detectedAssessment.confidence(),
  76:                     append(detectedAssessment.evidence(), "FIX-091 Replay persistence: detected=" + detected
  77:                             + ", candidate=" + candidate + ", confirmed=" + confirmed
  78:                             + ", candidateCount=" + count + "/" + requiredCandles + "."));
  79:         return new Decision(effective, detected, candidate, confirmed, count, promoted);
  80:     }
  81: 
  82:     private List<String> append(List<String> evidence, String value) {
  83:         java.util.ArrayList<String> result = new java.util.ArrayList<>(evidence == null ? List.of() : evidence);
  84:         result.add(value);
  85:         return List.copyOf(result);
  86:     }
  87: 
  88:     private record RegimeState(MarketRegime confirmed, MarketRegime candidate, int candidateCount, Instant lastCandleTime) {}
  89: 
  90:     public record Decision(MarketRegimeAssessment effectiveAssessment,
  91:                            MarketRegime detectedRegime,
  92:                            MarketRegime candidateRegime,
  93:                            MarketRegime confirmedRegime,
  94:                            int candidateCount,
  95:                            boolean promoted) {}
  96: }
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.Comparator;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.Optional;
  12: import java.util.HashSet;
  13: import java.util.Set;
  14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();
  75:         s.reference = observedAt;
  76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
  77:     }
  78: 
  79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
  80:         State s = required();
  81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
 176: 
 177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
 178: 
 179:     private static final class State {
 180:         final long runId;
 181:         final List<TradeSignal> signals;
 182:         final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
 183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
 184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
 185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
 186:         Instant reference;
 187:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
 188:         ReplayPrice latestPrice;
 189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
 190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
 191:             this.runId = runId;
 192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
 193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
 194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
 195:         }
 196:     }

  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  91:         }
  92: 
  93:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
  94:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
  95:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
  96:             audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  97:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY.");
  98:         }
  99: 
 100:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings.getExecutionProfile());
 101:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 102:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 103:             // FIX-112B: only the fresh direct-BUY BALANCED path receives the narrow
 104:             // 5m-NEUTRAL exception. validateBuyContext() and AGGRESSIVE fallback keep
 105:             // calling balancedBuy() directly so their proven Production behavior is unchanged.
 106:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuyWithNeutralFiveException(
 107:                     executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour(), entryQualityScore);
 108:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(executionSignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 109:         };
 110:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 111:         audit(executionSignal, "DIRECT_BUY", assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 112:         return result;
 113:     }
 114: 
 115:     /**
 116:      * FIX-021 / accumulated-evidence authority parity:
 117:      * Validate only the current 5m/1h execution authority using the same configured
 118:      * execution profile as a normal direct BUY. This intentionally skips the 1m BUY
 119:      * transition requirement because accumulated evidence may be triggered by a WATCH,
 120:      * but it must never be allowed to use weaker higher-timeframe authority than the
 121:      * normal BUY path would accept.
 122:      */
 123:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 124:         return validateBuyContext(referenceSignal, "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE");
 125:     }
 126: 
 127:     /** The caller path is audit evidence only; context eligibility remains identical. */
 128:     public ValidationResult validateBuyContext(TradeSignal referenceSignal, String auditPath) {
 129:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
 130:             return ValidationResult.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Execution reference signal is missing required timing data.");
 131:         }
 132: 
 133:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 134:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 135:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 136:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 137:         }
 138: 
 139:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
 140:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
 141:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
 142:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for accumulated-evidence authority.");
 143:         }
 144: 
 145:         HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment=htfAssessment.assess(fiveMinute,true,oneHour,true);
 146:         if(!assessment.allowed()) {
 147:             recordAssessmentMetric(assessment);
 148:             audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 149:             return ValidationResult.reject(assessment.code(), "FIX-154 higher-timeframe assessment rejected this BUY context.");
 150:         }
 151: 
 152:         ExecutionProfile profile = ExecutionProfile.from(settings().getExecutionProfile());
 153:         TradeSignal oneMinuteQuality = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, EXECUTION_INTERVAL);
 154:         TradeSignal qualitySignal = oneMinuteQuality == null ? referenceSignal : oneMinuteQuality;
 155:         ValidationResult profileResult = switch (profile) {
 156:             case CONSERVATIVE -> conservativeBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 157:             case BALANCED -> balancedBuy(assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 158:             case AGGRESSIVE -> aggressiveBuy(qualitySignal, assessment.effectiveFiveMinute(), assessment.effectiveOneHour());
 159:         };
 160:         ValidationResult result = cap(profileResult, assessment);
 161:         audit(referenceSignal, auditPath, assessment, result.code(), result.explanation());
 162:         return result;
 163:     }
 164: 
 165:     public ValidationResult validateSell(TradeSignal executionSignal) {
 166:         ValidationResult base = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, false);
 167:         if (!base.allowed()) return base;
 168: 
 169:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 170:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
 171:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
 361:         if (!buy && !isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
 362:             return ValidationResult.reject("NOT_SELL", "The 1m decision is not SELL/STRONG_SELL.");
 363:         }
 364:         return ValidationResult.allow(100, "BASE_VALID", "Base execution signal is valid.");
 365:     }
 366: 
 367:     private TradeSignal latestAtOrBefore(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
 368:         return latestSignal(signal.getSymbol(), interval, signal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 369:     }
 370: 
 371:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> previousSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 372:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.previousBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 373:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 374:     }
 375: 
 376:     private java.util.Optional<TradeSignal> latestSignal(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 377:         if (replayScope != null && replayScope.active()) return replayScope.latestAtOrBefore(symbol, interval, reference);
 378:         return signalRepository.findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByGeneratedAtDesc(symbol, interval, reference);
 379:     }
 380: 
 381:     private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
 382:         return signal != null
 383:                 && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
 384:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
 385:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
 386:     }
 387: 
 388:     private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 389:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 390:     }
 391: 
 392:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 393:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 394:     }
 395: 
 396:     private boolean isWatchOrNeutral(SignalDecision decision) {
 397:         return decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
 398:     }
 399: 
 400:     private WalletSettings settings() {
 401:         return settingsRepository.findById(1L)
 402:                 .orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 403:                         .id(1L)
 404:                         .executionProfile(ExecutionProfile.BALANCED.name())
 405:                         .requireNewBuyTransition(true)
 406:                         .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
 407:                         .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 408:                         .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
 409:                         .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
 410:                         .build());
 411:     }
 412: 
 413:     private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
 414:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
 415:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
 416:         if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();
 417:         return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
 418:     }
 419: 
 420:     /** Used only after progressive raw-HTF checks have already failed; never changes an add decision. */
 421:     public void recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(TradeSignal referenceSignal) {
 321:             String name,
 322:             BigDecimal activationPercent,
 323:             BigDecimal initialLockPercent,
 324:             BigDecimal trailStepPercent
 325:     ) {}

 328:         return settingsRepository.findById(1L).orElseGet(() -> settingsRepository.save(
 329:                 WalletSettings.builder()

 343:                         .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
 344:                         .updatedAt(Instant.now())
 345:                         .build()));
 346:     }

 348:     private BigDecimal nvl(BigDecimal value, BigDecimal fallback) {
 349:         return value == null ? fallback : value;

   8: ## 1. Business objective and flow
   9: 
  10: Replay must use current Production paper-trading policies with an isolated
  11: virtual account. It must never read Production account state, call HTTP, or
  12: call candle-only output historical parity.
  13: 
  14: 1. User selects one symbol, virtual profile, window and mode.
  15: 2. Qualifier validates every input before a generated Replay artifact exists.
  16: 3. A refusal writes only a run/certificate diagnostic.
  17: 4. Qualified analysis uses closed, identity-matching candles.
  18: 5. Simulation invokes pure Production policies with only virtual adapters.
  19: 6. Results stage privately and publish only on terminal success.
  20: 
  21: Release 1 labels outcomes only `ANALYSIS_PARITY`,
  22: `PRODUCTION_LOGIC_SIMULATION`, or `REFUSED_INSUFFICIENT_EVIDENCE`.
  23: `ENTRY_DECISION_PARITY` and historical wallet-lifecycle parity are out of scope:
  24: they require account-state evidence that Replay is forbidden to read.
  25: 
  26: ## 2. Root cause, evidence and scope
  27: 
  28: Replay currently can include open candles, has null IDs, reads
  29: `wallet_settings`, and differs from Production price/signal management.
  30: Evidence is current source in `RegressionTestWorker`,
  31: `ShadowProductionReplayService`, `ExecutionReplayScope`,
  32: `TradeExecutionValidationService`, `DynamicProfitLockService`,
  33: `LivePositionProtectionService`, `PositionManagementService`, and
  34: `PaperTradingService`.
  35: 
  36: Close-message lineage, deployed JAR/config identity and historical
  37: order-book/derivative/sentiment availability are not durable for all old
  38: windows. Strict claims are therefore unreachable for such eras unless a future
  39: approved forward-capture design is implemented.
  40:

  43: staging; resource limits; tests. Out: Production strategy change, real exchange,

  45: FIX-11K/FIX-11Q/pre-FIX122/EXPERIMENTAL. Those paths remain explicit
  46: `EXPERIMENTAL` and cannot enter baseline output.

## FINDINGS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — REJECT:** RPR-0.6 is not complete enough for dual-agent technical-design approval. Its declared “complete” registry and data boundary conflict with the current Replay entry point and output schema.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — REJECT:** The proposed no-account/no-HTTP boundary is not specified across all active dependency paths. It therefore cannot yet prove isolated virtual-account execution.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — REJECT:** The staging/publish design is not implementable safely from this specification: it lacks the required schema, transaction, publication, cancellation, and recovery contracts.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE — REJECT:** The design cannot yet support its `ANALYSIS_PARITY` label safely where historical configuration, dependency provenance, or ordering is absent.

## OBJECTIONS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The artifact fails the repository’s mandatory specification contract: it does not provide full paths, complete affected-file coverage, concrete new schema, or method-level inputs/outputs, transactionality, failure behavior, and concurrency for its new profile, certificate, staging, and virtual-account components.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The minimum revision must add `RegressionTestService`, request/controller validation, `MarketPriceEventService`, `WalletExecutionSizingPolicy`, `ExecutionPriceAuthorityService`, and every direct account/config/network dependency to the registry, or explicitly prove why each is untouched.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The revised contract must define immutable virtual-profile fields and hashes: execution profile, reserve, daily limits, profit-lock values, fee/slippage/fill model, exchange constraints, precision rules, configuration/version identity, and each evidence item’s as-of timestamp/provenance.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The revised contract must define a single terminal-state model. `FAILED_PROFILE_EVALUATION`, forbidden dependency, cancellation, refusal, and success cannot be left outside the stated three outcome labels.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** The revised contract must state exact atomic publication behavior: staging ownership, foreign keys, uniqueness/idempotency, run visibility, cancellation race handling, rollback behavior, and durable cleanup/recovery after process crash.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** Tests must add equal-timestamp price-event ordering and trace-ID preservation, all omitted-output-table publication checks, no-HTTP enforcement through the real Binance-constraint path, and an explicit historical configuration/provenance refusal matrix.

## UNKNOWNS

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** No production database, runtime, or external system was accessed.

- **AGENT INFERENCE:** I cannot establish whether historical windows possess sufficient persisted configuration/version and dependency provenance without a bounded database evidence review; RPR-0.6 must fail closed until its qualifier defines and verifies those conditions.

- **PROJECT EVIDENCE:** The reviewed artifact SHA-256 is `5C057C181459727E7D62F580F525FDB4392E15BBEAD7A27F5BAA2F3AAADF5A03`. Its Git baseline is the current `HEAD`, but the worktree is materially dirty; its proposed future re-hash is not a substitute for a complete design contract.

VERDICT: REJECTED
